# Functions

Code: one page per function of the game executable: what it does, whom it calls, and the state it reads and writes.

2044 records. Format of a record: [kinds/function.json](../../kinds/function.json). Data: [data/functions.jsonl](../../data/functions.jsonl).

| Name | Address | Area | Purpose |
|---|---|---|---|
| [Ability_GetAuxRecord](Ability_GetAuxRecord.md) | `0x140275A78` | [Ability data](../areas/ability-data.md) | Returns the executable's per-ability auxiliary record and an extra category-specific pointer for an ability id. |
| [Ability_GetJpCost](Ability_GetJpCost.md) | `0x1402CC0AC` | [Ability data](../areas/ability-data.md) | Returns the ability's JP cost: the u16 at Ability row +64 (jp_cost_low \| jp_cost_high << 8); 0xFFFF when the table or row is missing. |
| [AbilityAction_BuildWorkingCopy](AbilityAction_BuildWorkingCopy.md) | `0x1402BAE24` | [Ability data](../areas/ability-data.md) | Creates the writable ability action table: copies the executable's built-in table of 368 records (20 bytes each) and then overwrites fields from the OverrideAbilityActionData nxd table (id 247). |
| [AbilityAction_GetRecord](AbilityAction_GetRecord.md) | `0x1402BB060` | [Ability data](../areas/ability-data.md) | Returns a pointer to the 20-byte action record of an ability, building the working table on first use. |
| [AbilityTable_GetRow](AbilityTable_GetRow.md) | `0x1400F8474` | [Ability data](../areas/ability-data.md) | Returns the Ability NXD row for an ability id, or null when the table or row is missing. |
| [Action_BuildCurrentAction](Action_BuildCurrentAction.md) | `0x140320504` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Turns one packed four-byte action entry into the game's current-action block at `0x1418716A0`: it resolves the command, fills the action's range and presentation fields from the ability or item data, and for the item ... |
| [Action_PickTargetEquipment](Action_PickTargetEquipment.md) | `0x1403065C4` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Chooses which equipment slot a steal or break action aims at, from the target unit's hand and body slots, and reports the chosen slot and item to the action builder. |
| [Action_SetWeaponItem](Action_SetWeaponItem.md) | `0x1403098CC` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Fills the weapon item id of a battle action from the acting unit's equipped weapon, with 255 standing for empty hands and zero written for an empty or out-of-range id. |
| [AdditionalKerning_Lookup](AdditionalKerning_Lookup.md) | `0x1403BA2D4` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Looks up the (left, right) glyph pair in the .ker table's std::map and returns its extra (x, y) kerning (0 when missing); builds the map first when it is dirty. |
| [Ai_ActionWorthOnUnit](Ai_ActionWorthOnUnit.md) | `0x14038B34C` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Tells the AI whether the action in g_AiCurrentAction is worth trying on unit `slot`: 0 no, 1 yes, 2 yes (a stronger case). |
| [Ai_StatusLastsTurns](Ai_StatusLastsTurns.md) | `0x14038BAB4` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Tells the battle AI whether a unit has a status and will still have it after the given number of its turns. |
| [Ai_SurveyTargets](Ai_SurveyTargets.md) | `0x14031FD40` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Before planning, goes over every unit and every action in the acting unit's action list and notes what those actions could do to each unit (hurt it, help it), then marks which of its own actions are worth planning with. |
| [Ai_UnitTurnStep](Ai_UnitTurnStep.md) | `0x14031F130` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Runs one step of an AI-controlled unit's decision (several frames per turn) and, when it is done, writes the chosen move and action. |
| [App_LaunchClassic](App_LaunchClassic.md) | `0x1401DDB5C` | [Application and startup](../areas/app-startup.md) | Starts the Classic executable and ends this one. |
| [App_ProcessMessages](App_ProcessMessages.md) | `0x14002F728` | [Application and startup](../areas/app-startup.md) | Drains up to ten Win32 messages per call. |
| [App_RunFrame](App_RunFrame.md) | `0x14002A1F0` | [Application and startup](../areas/app-startup.md) | One iteration of the game's application loop: frame pacing, the debug/capture gate, Steam callbacks, the UI/render/audio update chain and the secondary frame-rate wait. |
| [AtlasShelf_Alloc](AtlasShelf_Alloc.md) | `0x1403B6D68` | [Graphics](../areas/graphics.md) | Shelf (row) allocator for the layer atlas page: places a rectangle plus margin and returns the packed integer rectangle. |
| [AudioBus_FindByName](AudioBus_FindByName.md) | `0x14044DFD4` | [Resource](../areas/resource.md) | Returns the mixer bus number for a bus name, or 0xFFFF when there is none. |
| [AudioBus_FindMasterOut](AudioBus_FindMasterOut.md) | `0x14044E07C` | [Resource](../areas/resource.md) | Returns the bus number of the master output. |
| [AudioDsp_InitCoefficientSets](AudioDsp_InitCoefficientSets.md) | `0x1404A203C` | [Resource](../areas/resource.md) | Initialises the work area of an audio DSP unit and fills its per-channel coefficient pointer slots. |
| [BannerWord_Draw](BannerWord_Draw.md) | `0x140227AD8` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Builds the gradient quads of one banner word image from its run of sprite cells. |
| [BannerWords_FillQuads](BannerWords_FillQuads.md) | `0x1402DF154` | [Event system](../areas/event-system.md) | Fills the gradient quads of one banner (a row of word cells) from the legacy banner tables. |
| [BarTop_Setup](BarTop_Setup.md) | `0x14019F330` | [Errands](../areas/errands.md) | Sets up the tavern's top menu (rumors, errands): the errand row is open only when situation 3220 holds. |
| [Battle_ActorResultLevels](Battle_ActorResultLevels.md) | `0x14030C704` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Applies the acting unit's action result and returns, in four bytes, what the level-up banners need: two result bytes, the new unit level and the new job level. |
| [Battle_AITurnPrepare](Battle_AITurnPrepare.md) | `0x14031F534` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Fills the per-unit AI table (visibility, HP/MP ratios, flags) and then runs one AI turn. |
| [Battle_AITurnRun](Battle_AITurnRun.md) | `0x14038A468` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Runs one AI trial: applies the candidate action on the preview unit table, lets the turn engine play on (up to 255 steps), scores the outcome and restores the engine state. |
| [Battle_ApplyActionResult](Battle_ApplyActionResult.md) | `0x14030A488` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Applies the pending action result of one unit to that unit: HP/MP damage and healing, stat changes, Brave/Faith changes, EXP/level changes, status sets/clears and the death check. |
| [Battle_AwardActorJp](Battle_AwardActorJp.md) | `0x14030C7C4` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Gives the acting battle unit its JP for the current job and reports whether its job level changed (for the level-up message). |
| [Battle_AwardUnitJp](Battle_AwardUnitJp.md) | `0x14028338C` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Awards job JP to a battle unit after an action: it validates the unit, resolves its job index and JP amount, applies the award and reports the amount. |
| [Battle_BuildEntryUnits](Battle_BuildEntryUnits.md) | `0x14030428C` | [Unit data](../areas/unit-data.md) | Builds the battle units of the current battle from its 16 entries: fetches the entry block, lets the battle's OverrideEntryData rows overwrite the entries, then places the units. |
| [Battle_BuildRosterTeam](Battle_BuildRosterTeam.md) | `0x140270CE4` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Fills the live 21-slot battle unit table (`g_BattleUnitTable`, `0x141853CE0`, stride 512) from a 5-entry spawn list, one team at a time. |
| [Battle_CommandTask](Battle_CommandTask.md) | `0x140202B08` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | The per-frame player battle command task: advances the turn engine, claims the active unit, and puts up the command menu (Move / Abilities / Wait / Status) or hands the unit to the AI. |
| [Battle_GetEntryBlock](Battle_GetEntryBlock.md) | `0x14028416C` | [Unit data](../areas/unit-data.md) | The 16 spawn entries (40 bytes each, 640 bytes) of the current battle inside the loaded entry data. |
| [Battle_Init](Battle_Init.md) | `0x14020EF10` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Allocates and resets the battle singletons when a battle starts: BattleControl, post-battle calculator, a 3008-byte battle object, the map-view camera manager, markers and the command menu. |
| [Battle_InitScene](Battle_InitScene.md) | `0x1402C8818` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | The battle scene initialiser started by event opcode 0x78 mode 7: it builds the battle UI, places the units and their markers, shows the cursor and waits for the battle to end. |
| [Battle_IsTileOccupied](Battle_IsTileOccupied.md) | `0x140282A7C` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Tests a map tile for the targeting cursor: bounds, terrain passability, the unit standing there and the large-monster exception for jobs 94..96. |
| [Battle_JoinUnitAndLoad](Battle_JoinUnitAndLoad.md) | `0x1401F2E4C` | [Graphics](../areas/graphics.md) | The mid-battle join: runs Battle_AddJoinedUnit, then the look loader once per frame until it returns 0. |
| [Battle_MenuClose](Battle_MenuClose.md) | `0x1402100F4` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Closes the command menu for the acting unit. |
| [Battle_MenuHidePage](Battle_MenuHidePage.md) | `0x140154308` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Hides the page for a menu id (or all six pages when `menuId == -1`). |
| [Battle_MenuOpen](Battle_MenuOpen.md) | `0x140210184` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Opens the command menu for the acting unit. |
| [Battle_MenuPageOfCommand](Battle_MenuPageOfCommand.md) | `0x140154248` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Maps a battle command/menu id to one of the six `BattleMenu` page numbers. |
| [Battle_MenuShowPage](Battle_MenuShowPage.md) | `0x140154624` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Shows one of the six command-menu pages and binds its radio buttons, icon and title. |
| [Battle_MoveRange_Build](Battle_MoveRange_Build.md) | `0x14027B2F0` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Builds the move range of one unit: sets up the scratch block, seeds the unit's tile with its Move budget, spreads the budget tile by tile and jump by jump, then commits the tiles the unit can stop on. |
| [Battle_MoveRange_CeilingAbove](Battle_MoveRange_CeilingAbove.md) | `0x14027C348` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | The lowest underside, over both map layers of one column, that lies above a given height on one edge; 127 when there is none. |
| [Battle_MoveRange_CheckMount](Battle_MoveRange_CheckMount.md) | `0x14027C218` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | For a target tile holding a mountable ally: records in the tile's mark byte 3 the budget with which the unit could get onto the mount. |
| [Battle_MoveRange_CheckSource](Battle_MoveRange_CheckSource.md) | `0x14027BAC4` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Prepares leaving the current tile in one direction: the height of the edge being left, the headroom above it and the budget record to spend from; returns non-zero when the tile cannot be left that way. |
| [Battle_MoveRange_CheckStep](Battle_MoveRange_CheckStep.md) | `0x14027BD0C` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Tests one target of a step or jump (variant bit 0 = map layer, variant >= 2 = onto the unit standing there) and, when the unit can get there with a better budget, stores the budget and flags the tile as frontier. |
| [Battle_MoveRange_Commit](Battle_MoveRange_Commit.md) | `0x14027B7F0` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Turns the budgets into the lit set: decides for each of the 512 tiles whether the unit can stop there, counts them and writes the result to the tile table (bit 0x20 of tile byte +5) or to the marking table. |
| [Battle_MoveRange_Expand](Battle_MoveRange_Expand.md) | `0x14027C138` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Spreads the budget from the current frontier tile to its four neighbours. |
| [Battle_MoveRange_ExpandJump](Battle_MoveRange_ExpandJump.md) | `0x14027C5E0` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Spreads the budget from the current frontier tile by horizontal jumps: in each of the four straight lines, over 1 up to Jump / 2 tiles. |
| [Battle_MoveRange_GapBlocked](Battle_MoveRange_GapBlocked.md) | `0x14027C3DC` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Whether a column stops a horizontal jump that passes over it at a given height: anything (tile body or enemy) overlapping the band from the height to height + body blocks, and so does a column that is entirely above. |
| [Battle_MoveRange_InitTiles](Battle_MoveRange_InitTiles.md) | `0x14027B530` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Clears the range marks, gives every tile its movement flag byte (slope, standable, blocked), stamps the other units onto their tiles and seeds the unit's own tile with Move + 1. |
| [Battle_MoveRange_OutOfBounds](Battle_MoveRange_OutOfBounds.md) | `0x14027BA88` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | True when the target coordinates in the scratch (two signed words) are outside the map. |
| [Battle_MoveRange_WeatherCost](Battle_MoveRange_WeatherCost.md) | `0x14027AD6C` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | The movement cost of the weather-dependent terrains: 1, 2 or 3 from event variable 35 and two bits of dword_140D3A830. |
| [Battle_MoveRangeSetup](Battle_MoveRangeSetup.md) | `0x14027ADB8` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Prepares the globals the move-range expansion reads: it copies the acting unit move, jump, stance and status data into the range scratch block and fills the sixteen-entry blocker table. |
| [Battle_PlaceEntryUnits](Battle_PlaceEntryUnits.md) | `0x14027128C` | [Unit data](../areas/unit-data.md) | Builds battle unit i (g_PreviewUnitTable + 512 x i) from entry i for the 16 entries of a battle, decides who is placed and queues each placed unit's look. |
| [Battle_PreviewUnitAction](Battle_PreviewUnitAction.md) | `0x140282BDC` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Rebuilds the preview of the action a battle unit currently holds: it resets the per-unit target flags, resolves the action mode and range, and refills the preview block. |
| [Battle_Rand15](Battle_Rand15.md) | `0x14030D088` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | The battle's random number: a 15-bit value, fixed while a forecast runs. |
| [Battle_ResolveAction](Battle_ResolveAction.md) | `0x1403099B0` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Resolves one action request against one target: selects the formula from the action type / ability, runs the formula handler and leaves a 40-byte result record for the apply step. |
| [Battle_ResolveReaction](Battle_ResolveReaction.md) | `0x140283C00` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Resolves a reaction or secondary action: it copies the action, rebuilds the preview and range, calls the action resolver and then the reaction bookkeeping. |
| [Battle_RestoreCapture](Battle_RestoreCapture.md) | `0x1402CE7A8` | [Save data](../areas/save-data.md) | Restores the five memory blocks and the live state of a saved battle payload into the running scene, excluding the scene-task block that the game never restored. |
| [Battle_RestoreSceneVars](Battle_RestoreSceneVars.md) | `0x1402CE5C8` | [Save data](../areas/save-data.md) | Puts a saved battle's scene state back from the battle snapshot region: seven scene variables, the live stores, the battle unit table, the look queue and the look pool. |
| [Battle_ResultResetStatusSets](Battle_ResultResetStatusSets.md) | `0x14030A43C` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Clears the two pending status sets of an action-result block (and a five-byte scratch copy in the battle formula state). |
| [Battle_SaveCapture](Battle_SaveCapture.md) | `0x1402CDBC4` | [Save data](../areas/save-data.md) | Captures the battle state into the autosave battle payload: the scene variables, the five memory blocks A..E, the live item and unit state and the item order record. |
| [Battle_ScaleByDifficulty](Battle_ScaleByDifficulty.md) | `0x140309FB8` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Scales the HP and MP damage of a resolved action by the play mode's percentages: the row of the DifficultyLevel table that the configuration names, one percentage for one side of the target and another for the other. |
| [Battle_ShowJobLevelUp](Battle_ShowJobLevelUp.md) | `0x1402041D8` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Shows the job level-up banner over the acting unit when its action raised its job level. |
| [Battle_ShowUnitLevelUp](Battle_ShowUnitLevelUp.md) | `0x14020401C` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Shows the level-up banner over the acting unit when its action raised its unit level. |
| [Battle_SnapshotLiveState](Battle_SnapshotLiveState.md) | `0x140279BF4` | [Save data](../areas/save-data.md) | Captures or restores the live save state the battle image carries: the item stores and the unit save records, copied between the live globals and a save work block in the direction the second argument selects. |
| [Battle_StatusApply](Battle_StatusApply.md) | `0x14030CAA8` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Converts the pending status sets of one unit's action result block into applied status bits, after filtering them against the unit's innate/immunity sets. |
| [Battle_StatusRefresh](Battle_StatusRefresh.md) | `0x14030CA00` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Applies the pending status clears of a unit and recomputes the effective 40-bit status set. |
| [Battle_StatusResolve](Battle_StatusResolve.md) | `0x14030C87C` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Applies the pending status adds and removes of one battle unit: filters them, clears, sets, then settles the effects that depend on the new set. |
| [Battle_TimelineBuildList](Battle_TimelineBuildList.md) | `0x1402333E8` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Builds the 40-entry timeline list for the UI and overlays the acting unit's current command preview. |
| [Battle_TimelineRefresh](Battle_TimelineRefresh.md) | `0x140215E54` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Refreshes the Combat Timeline panel: builds the forecast, copies it into `g_TurnList`, and fills the visible rows (portrait, name, HP/MP, CT) and the scroll position. |
| [Battle_TurnEngine](Battle_TurnEngine.md) | `0x14030F030` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | The battle turn state machine: ticks CT, picks the next unit, starts and finishes turns, and resolves charged actions. |
| [Battle_TurnStep](Battle_TurnStep.md) | `0x1402029DC` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Runs one step of the turn engine on the player path and dispatches the returned action code to the scene/UI. |
| [Battle_UnitStatusFill](Battle_UnitStatusFill.md) | `0x1402158F4` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Fills the battle status panel's view record (and the addon's cached fields) from the live unit table. |
| [BattleAct_BuildResult](BattleAct_BuildResult.md) | `0x140281CE8` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Resolves the unit's queued action against its targets and fills the scene object's result record (obj +0x1A8): target list, flags and the effect word +0x14 the presentation is keyed by. |
| [BattleAct_NoEffectStep](BattleAct_NoEffectStep.md) | `0x140206758` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Applies the resolved action to every target and starts each target's hit motion: the step that turns the formula results into HP, MP and status changes and visible hits. |
| [BattleAct_SetupEffect](BattleAct_SetupEffect.md) | `0x1402060F4` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Selects the visual effect for an ability: builds the list of units on screen, reads the ability's charge byte from `g_AbilityAnim`, combines it with the ability element and calls the effect-number selector. |
| [BattleAct_Step](BattleAct_Step.md) | `0x140206A0C` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | The scene step that executes the acting object's action: builds the result, sets the effect word and chooses the presentation path. |
| [BattleAction_ApplyRequest](BattleAction_ApplyRequest.md) | `0x140281434` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Wrapper that takes a request whose first byte is a unit slot, points at that unit action block, and applies the request; on success in the normal battle state it also refreshes the unit action. |
| [BattleAction_BuildUnitRequest](BattleAction_BuildUnitRequest.md) | `0x140283280` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Writes a command and action word into a unit action block, optionally marks the ring, and applies the request through the common wrapper. |
| [BattleAction_GetRangeInfo](BattleAction_GetRangeInfo.md) | `0x140281C24` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Chooses how an action acquires its range and area: the item path for the item-like command kinds and the ability record path otherwise, filling the caller flags and returning the range result. |
| [BattleAction_RunScript](BattleAction_RunScript.md) | `0x140234B3C` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | The driver task for the battle presentation programs: reads the current script id from scene block 1, starts up to four sub-tasks defined by that script's 64-byte record, waits for them and repeats, then ends. |
| [BattleAction_StartScript](BattleAction_StartScript.md) | `0x140234A94` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Starts one of the seven battle presentation programs (the "scene script" table) and records the acting unit and target for the tasks that program starts. |
| [BattleAnim_PickProjectile](BattleAnim_PickProjectile.md) | `0x1401FAD94` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Chooses the projectile of a throw motion by the effect word. |
| [BattleAnim_SelectMotion](BattleAnim_SelectMotion.md) | `0x140269988` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Chooses the battle motion (animation id) for an ability: weapon attack, cast/ranged special, or a fixed motion from the per-ability table, then starts it on the unit. |
| [BattleAnim_SelectWeaponMotion](BattleAnim_SelectWeaponMotion.md) | `0x140269790` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Chooses the motion of a plain weapon attack from the attacker's weapon animation type and, for some types, from how high the target stands, then starts it. |
| [BattleAnim_SetChargeMotion](BattleAnim_SetChargeMotion.md) | `0x140269920` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Sets the charging motion from the charge byte of g_AbilityAnim (the effect word +0x142). |
| [BattleAnim_StartMotion](BattleAnim_StartMotion.md) | `0x140268E04` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Starts a motion on a battle unit's scene object: stores the animation number and the facing value the unit's animation will use. |
| [BattleCommand_BuildMenu](BattleCommand_BuildMenu.md) | `0x1402EAD14` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | The battle command task: it reads the cell text of the command menu, starts the list and screen tasks, and runs the menu until the unit action is chosen. |
| [BattleCommand_BuildScreen](BattleCommand_BuildScreen.md) | `0x1402E600C` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Builds the battle command/ability screen: the unit panels, the job record, the ability list entries and the equip detail panel the player navigates during a turn. |
| [BattleCommand_BuildUnitList](BattleCommand_BuildUnitList.md) | `0x1402EB174` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Builds the command list for the active unit: it asks the list builder for the commands, resolves their text and writes the cell words the menu reads. |
| [BattleCommand_CommitCursor](BattleCommand_CommitCursor.md) | `0x1402EB874` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Closes a battle command list: normalises its entries and stores the cursor row. |
| [BattleCommand_StoreCursorRow](BattleCommand_StoreCursorRow.md) | `0x1402EB828` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Remembers the cursor row of the current battle command menu for the acting unit. |
| [BattleControl_FrameUpdate](BattleControl_FrameUpdate.md) | `0x1401EA110` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Per-frame update of the battle control object; its first part sets the fast-forward level in battle straight from the held buttons. |
| [BattleFormation_DrawUnits](BattleFormation_DrawUnits.md) | `0x140216EB4` | [Graphics](../areas/graphics.md) | The battle formation draw (called from sub_140213F14): per unit index 0..20 it reads the CharShape row of the scene object's sprite byte for the draw height. |
| [BattleLabel_ShowReactionSignal](BattleLabel_ShowReactionSignal.md) | `0x14014BFFC` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Draws a plain text label (a BattleSignal title) over a unit, in the TextReaction node. |
| [BattleLabel_ShowStatusSignal](BattleLabel_ShowStatusSignal.md) | `0x14014B87C` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Draws a styled status label over a unit: the BattleSignal row's title with the icon of the status that names the row. |
| [BattleLoad_StepFresh](BattleLoad_StepFresh.md) | `0x1403044EC` | [Graphics](../areas/graphics.md) | State machine of one battle scene load: prepares, builds the roster queue, loads every queued look through CharaPalette_ReloadForShape and starts the battle turn engine. |
| [BattleMain_SetPanelJobRecord](BattleMain_SetPanelJobRecord.md) | `0x14010141C` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Stores a job record (the first 92 bytes) into one of the battle main screen's two unit panel blocks. |
| [BattleMenu_ConfirmRow](BattleMenu_ConfirmRow.md) | `0x140237B18` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Acts on a confirmed battle command menu row: picks the follow-up screen code and starts the scene task that shows it. |
| [BattleMenu_RunStep](BattleMenu_RunStep.md) | `0x1402381C4` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Runs one step of the battle menu step table: sets the current menu and starts the step's task. |

| [Formula_77_PhysicalHPDrain](Formula_77_PhysicalHPDrain.md) | `0x140308D48` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Physical HP drain: a magic-style hit check then percentage-of-max-HP damage that heals the caster. |
| [Formula_78_MAYPhysicalFullChain](Formula_78_MAYPhysicalFullChain.md) | `0x140308DBC` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | MA x Y physical: the full magic chain applied to a physical-style attack. |
| [Formula_79_PhysicalMissingHP](Formula_79_PhysicalMissingHP.md) | `0x140308DF4` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Physical plus missing HP: a physical evade check and magic chain, then damage equal to the caster's missing HP. |
| [Formula_80_PhysicalStatus](Formula_80_PhysicalStatus.md) | `0x140308E50` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Physical plus status: a physical evade check and magic chain, then a status through F56. |
| [Formula_81_MonsterBuffHealMagicHitStatus](Formula_81_MonsterBuffHealMagicHitStatus.md) | `0x140308E90` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Status with a magic chance: MA + X with the element boost, the caster's magic modifiers and zodiac (no Faith); on success the ability's status through Formula_56. |
| [Formula_82_SelfDestructHPBasedDamage](Formula_82_SelfDestructHPBasedDamage.md) | `0x140308EE4` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Self-destruct: HP-based damage, with a different value for self versus area targets. |
| [Formula_83_MonsterHPDamageMagicChain](Formula_83_MonsterHPDamageMagicChain.md) | `0x140308F28` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Monster % HP damage: a magic hit check then percentage-of-target-max-HP damage. |
| [Formula_84_MPRestorationMagicBased](Formula_84_MPRestorationMagicBased.md) | `0x140308FA0` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | MP restoration: MA x Y MP healing with caster boost and Zodiac; always succeeds. |
| [Formula_85_PeckPhysicalHitPADrain](Formula_85_PeckPhysicalHitPADrain.md) | `0x140308FF8` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Peck: a magic hit that reduces the target's PA. |
| [Formula_86_BeamPhysicalHitMADrain](Formula_86_BeamPhysicalHitMADrain.md) | `0x14030902C` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Beam: a magic hit that reduces the target's MA. |
| [Formula_87_BequeathBaconSelfSacrifice](Formula_87_BequeathBaconSelfSacrifice.md) | `0x140309060` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Sacrifice: fills the target's HP and MP words with its own maximums, then, when the target is below level 99, turns the result into a stat change (flag word 0x80) and applies the ability's status to the caster. |
| [Formula_88_MalboroSporesMagicHitRecruitment](Formula_88_MalboroSporesMagicHitRecruitment.md) | `0x1403090F4` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Malboro Spores: a magic hit check that validates the target and sets the recruit/charm flag. |
| [Formula_89_LevelDrainPhysicalHitLevelSteal](Formula_89_LevelDrainPhysicalHitLevelSteal.md) | `0x14030918C` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Level Drain: a magic hit that sets the level-drain flag. |
| [Formula_90_DragonSCharmSpeciesGatedStatus](Formula_90_DragonSCharmSpeciesGatedStatus.md) | `0x1403091D8` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Dragon's Charm: a species-gated status with no hit check. |
| [Formula_91_DragonSGiftSpeciesGatedHPMPGift](Formula_91_DragonSGiftSpeciesGatedHPMPGift.md) | `0x140309214` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Dragon's Gift: a species-gated self-damage plus target heal. |
| [Formula_92_DragonSMightSpeciesGatedAllStatBoost](Formula_92_DragonSMightSpeciesGatedAllStatBoost.md) | `0x140309288` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Dragon's Might: a species-gated Brave/PA/MA/Speed boost. |
| [Formula_93_DragonSSpeedSpeciesGatedAllBuff](Formula_93_DragonSSpeedSpeciesGatedAllBuff.md) | `0x1403092D8` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Dragon's Speed: a species-gated all-buff. |
| [Formula_94_TriElementReDispatchesToF30](Formula_94_TriElementReDispatchesToF30.md) | `0x140307C10` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Tri-Element: shares the F30 target; the ability supplies the element and parameters. |
| [Formula_95_NanoflareReDispatchesToF30](Formula_95_NanoflareReDispatchesToF30.md) | `0x140307C10` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Nanoflare: shares the F30 target. |
| [Formula_96_MagicAveraged](Formula_96_MagicAveraged.md) | `0x140309310` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Averaged magic damage: A = MA, B = (MA + Y) / 2, then the magic damage tail (a thunk to Formula_MagicAvgDamage). |
| [Formula_97_MagicBasedBraveModify](Formula_97_MagicBasedBraveModify.md) | `0x140309318` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Magic-based Brave modify: a magic hit check that adjusts the target's Brave. |
| [Formula_98_PhysicalBasedBraveModify](Formula_98_PhysicalBasedBraveModify.md) | `0x14030934C` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Brave change with a magic roll that ignores Faith: the magic evade gate, then a MA + X chance; on a hit the target's Brave changes by Y. |
| [Formula_99_SpeedTimesWeaponPower](Formula_99_SpeedTimesWeaponPower.md) | `0x140309380` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Thrown weapon: damage = the caster's Speed x the weapon's WP through the physical modifiers; a target with the catch reaction may catch it on a Brave roll. |
| [Formula_BardDancerGate](Formula_BardDancerGate.md) | `0x140304D5C` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | The Bard and Dancer hit roll: scales the shown percent by X and rolls a miss against X. |
| [Formula_CasterMagMods](Formula_CasterMagMods.md) | `0x140305CB0` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | The caster's magic damage modifiers: two passive bits scale A. |
| [Formula_CasterStatusMods](Formula_CasterStatusMods.md) | `0x140305D08` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | The caster's status modifiers on A: Berserk raises it, Toad flattens it. |
| [Formula_ChanceRoll](Formula_ChanceRoll.md) | `0x140306408` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Turns R+6 (a chance in percent) into the shown hit percent and the roll that can make the action miss. |
| [Formula_ChanceSum](Formula_ChanceSum.md) | `0x140305F4C` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Builds a chance in percent in R+6: A plus the zodiac-adjusted B. |
| [Formula_CritRoll](Formula_CritRoll.md) | `0x140305EB4` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | The 4 percent critical hit: marks R+1, adds a random share of A and runs the knockback calculation. |
| [Formula_DamageAB](Formula_DamageAB.md) | `0x140305F08` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | The damage amount: R+6 = A * B capped at 0x7FFF, kind bit 0x80. |
| [Formula_DrainHP](Formula_DrainHP.md) | `0x1403062F0` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | HP drain: the target takes the amount and the caster gets it, or the reverse when the target is Undead. |
| [Formula_DrainMP](Formula_DrainMP.md) | `0x1403063B8` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | MP drain: the amount becomes MP damage on the target and an MP heal on the caster. |
| [Formula_DrawOutGate](Formula_DrawOutGate.md) | `0x14030655C` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | The Draw Out katana break roll: writes the caster-side result with the broken-or-kept flag. |
| [Formula_EarthFloatAndAffinity](Formula_EarthFloatAndAffinity.md) | `0x1403061C8` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Float nullifies an earth hit, then the element affinity step runs with the ability's element. |
| [Formula_ElemBoostAction](Formula_ElemBoostAction.md) | `0x140305BE8` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Element boost from the ability's element: A = 5 * A / 4 when the caster's element mask has the ability record's element. |
| [Formula_ElemBoostWeapon](Formula_ElemBoostWeapon.md) | `0x140305BB8` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Element boost from the weapon's element: A = 5 * A / 4 when the caster's element mask has the weapon's element. |
| [Formula_ElementAffinity](Formula_ElementAffinity.md) | `0x140305554` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | The target's element affinities applied to R+6: absorb, null, half, weak and the Oil double. |
| [Formula_EvadeByteRoll](Formula_EvadeByteRoll.md) | `0x140305130` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | One evade roll of Formula_EvadeRoll: a failed hit stores the miss reason. |
| [Formula_EvadeRoll](Formula_EvadeRoll.md) | `0x14030588C` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | The shared evade step of the physical and magic gates: scales the shown hit percent by the four evade bytes and rolls each of them. |
| [Formula_FaithScale](Formula_FaithScale.md) | `0x140306258` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Scales R+6 by the caster's and the target's faith: R+6 = R+6 * casterFaith * targetFaith / 10000. |
| [Formula_FinalizeAbsorb](Formula_FinalizeAbsorb.md) | `0x140306224` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Sets the kind bits of a damage result and turns an absorbed hit into a heal. |
| [Formula_HealSwap](Formula_HealSwap.md) | `0x140306378` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Turns the computed amount into a heal, or into damage when the target is Undead. |
| [Formula_KnockbackCalc](Formula_KnockbackCalc.md) | `0x140304DC0` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Works out the tile a hit target is knocked back to and queues it as a request. |
| [Formula_MagicAvgDamage](Formula_MagicAvgDamage.md) | `0x140306F9C` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Sets A = MA and B = (MA + Y) / 2, then jumps into the magic damage tail. |
| [Formula_MagicDamageTail](Formula_MagicDamageTail.md) | `0x140306F4C` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | The tail of every stock magic damage formula: the magic modifier chain on A, the damage, the scene and affinity steps, and the status tail. |

| [Formula_MagicHitNoElem](Formula_MagicHitNoElem.md) | `0x140306FCC` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Magic hit chance without the element boost: A = MA, B = X, then the magic modifier chain, the faith factor and the chance roll. |
| [Formula_MagicHitNoFaith](Formula_MagicHitNoFaith.md) | `0x140307088` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Magic hit chance without the faith factor: A = MA, B = X, then the element boost, the magic modifier chain and the chance roll. |
| [Formula_PhysAttackFull](Formula_PhysAttackFull.md) | `0x140306EF0` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | The whole stock weapon attack as one unit (formulas 1 and 2): weapon stat, bonus, modifiers, critical, damage, affinity. |
| [Formula_PhysMods](Formula_PhysMods.md) | `0x140306E34` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | The full physical modifier chain on A: attacker passives, then Berserk / Toad, the target's passives and statuses, and the zodiac step. |
| [Formula_PhysStatusHit](Formula_PhysStatusHit.md) | `0x1403070E4` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Physical status chance: runs the physical caster and target modifier chain on A, adds the zodiac step and rolls the chance. |
| [Formula_ReactionPrecheck](Formula_ReactionPrecheck.md) | `0x14030AF88` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Gate of the reaction-style handlers (Speechcraft, Throw): decides whether the reaction roll applies. |
| [Formula_RevivePrecheck](Formula_RevivePrecheck.md) | `0x140309504` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Revive gate: runs the status tail and continues only when a status is pending or the target is Undead. |
| [Formula_SceneVarElemMod](Formula_SceneVarElemMod.md) | `0x140306134` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Scene-variable element modifier on R+6: damage of some elements is cut or raised by the battle's scene variable 35. |
| [Formula_StatusChance19](Formula_StatusChance19.md) | `0x140306C10` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | The chance that a status effect goes with a damage hit: a 19 percent roll (Nanoflare-style id 219 uses a MA difference) that sets R+18 bit 0x200. |
| [Formula_StatusTailKeepHit](Formula_StatusTailKeepHit.md) | `0x140306944` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Runs the status application on the inflict record and restores the hit fields it may have touched. |
| [Formula_StealGate](Formula_StealGate.md) | `0x14030647C` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | The steal and break gate: turns the picked equipment into no effect when it is one of four protected ids or the target is protected. |
| [Formula_StealGilApply](Formula_StealGilApply.md) | `0x140305FA8` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Gil theft result: the caster gains and the target loses an amount from level, Speed and Y. |
| [Formula_TargetPassiveMag](Formula_TargetPassiveMag.md) | `0x140305D98` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | The target's passive modifiers against a magic hit. |
| [Formula_TargetPassivePhys](Formula_TargetPassivePhys.md) | `0x140305D40` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | The target's passive modifiers against a physical hit. |
| [Formula_TargetStatusMag](Formula_TargetStatusMag.md) | `0x140305E64` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | The target's status modifiers against a magic hit. |
| [Formula_TargetStatusPhys](Formula_TargetStatusPhys.md) | `0x140305DF0` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | The target's status modifiers against a physical hit. |
| [Formula_ZeroResult](Formula_ZeroResult.md) | `0x140305504` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | The stock no-effect result: nothing happens and the reason is 5 (nullified). |
| [Formula_Zodiac](Formula_Zodiac.md) | `0x140305178` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | The zodiac compatibility step on A: raises or lowers it by the pair of signs. |

| [FrequentUtilRenderer_CreateResources](FrequentUtilRenderer_CreateResources.md) | `0x1403AAA84` | [Graphics](../areas/graphics.md) | FrequentUtilRenderer set-up: loads shader/FrequentUtil.tec, creates six small constant buffers, loads 16 blue-noise textures, builds a 36-index buffer, looks up 18 texture slots and 10 constant-buffer slots, then crea... |
| [Game_Main](Game_Main.md) | `0x14002BD50` | [Application and startup](../areas/app-startup.md) | The game's WinMain body: it rejects a debugger at startup, sets the working directory, creates the splash/window objects, runs the application loop until exit, and tears the objects down. |
| [GameData_ReadSectors](GameData_ReadSectors.md) | `0x1402F40C4` | [Event system](../areas/event-system.md) | The game's general data read entry point, expressed in the original disc layout: it reads a run of 2048-byte sectors of one kind of game file, and sends event scripts (kind 17) to EventScript_LoadFile. |
| [GameDataPack_Open](GameDataPack_Open.md) | `0x1402F3A3C` | [Resource](../areas/resource.md) | Opens system/ffto/fftpack.bin once and loads its two tables of 4-byte entries (offsets and sizes) that GameData_ReadSectors indexes by file number. |
| [GameState_ResetJobFlags](GameState_ResetJobFlags.md) | `0x14028433C` | [Job data](../areas/job-data.md) | New-game reset that initialises byte_1411A7490: 125 per-jobtype flags (at +21) to 1 and 21 per-generic-job flags (at +0) to (GeneralJob.Unknown78 == 0), then clears a block of party/story state. |
| [GameState_ResetParty](GameState_ResetParty.md) | `0x140284500` | [Unit data](../areas/unit-data.md) | Resets the party-wide item state for a new game or a partial reset: the roster markers, the inventory counts, the Poachers' Den stock, the item badge block and the poach arrays, then the starting inventory and gil. |
| [GameTasks_RunFrameSteps](GameTasks_RunFrameSteps.md) | `0x1402574E0` | [Application and startup](../areas/app-startup.md) | Runs the game's task threads for one displayed frame: the whole per-frame step is repeated GlobalWork_GetSpeedMultiplier times, which is how fast-forward speeds the game up. |
| [GaussianKernel_Build](GaussianKernel_Build.md) | `0x1403ABD50` | [Graphics](../areas/graphics.md) | Builds the normalised one-sided 64-entry Gaussian weight table for the UI blur and returns the tap count. |
| [GlobalWork_GetFastForwardMode](GlobalWork_GetFastForwardMode.md) | `0x1401E8A9C` | [Application and startup](../areas/app-startup.md) | Returns the fast-forward mode of the current frame after the 'Fast-forward' setting is applied: 0 none, 1 battle, 2 event script, 3 world map. |
| [GlobalWork_GetSpeedMultiplier](GlobalWork_GetSpeedMultiplier.md) | `0x1401E8AC4` | [Application and startup](../areas/app-startup.md) | The number of game steps per displayed frame: 1 normally, 3 at fast-forward level 1, 5 at level 2. |
| [Glyph_GetAdvance](Glyph_GetAdvance.md) | `0x140331E60` | [Data access](../areas/data.md) | Returns the pixel advance of a message glyph. |
| [GradientWindow_ColourFromPaletteA](GradientWindow_ColourFromPaletteA.md) | `0x140220640` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Colours the 12 vertices of a gradient window from palette A. |
| [GradientWindow_ColourFromPaletteB](GradientWindow_ColourFromPaletteB.md) | `0x1402206A8` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | As GradientWindow_ColourFromPaletteA with palette B (0x142FEC4F0). |
| [GraphicsRenderer_AppendShaderRecord](GraphicsRenderer_AppendShaderRecord.md) | `0x1403AA8BC` | [Graphics](../areas/graphics.md) | Renderer vtable slot 8 (shared by SDFFontRenderer and FrequentUtilRenderer): appends a 16-byte record {r+24, 0} (the renderer's shader) to a caller-supplied vector. |
| [GridAvailability_Reset](GridAvailability_Reset.md) | `0x1402FC6D8` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Resets the availability flags of the grid screen entries. |
| [GridScreen_Task](GridScreen_Task.md) | `0x1402FC870` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Task of the legacy grid screen screen: a grid of five columns of unit portraits with a cursor and a help window. |
| [GUIAddon_OnStateEvent](GUIAddon_OnStateEvent.md) | `0x14040A664` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Shared owner handler for the keyframe events (type 40) a state posts: acts on the state entry the event points at (sound triggers, nested state links, state resets, part triggers). |
| [GUIBounds_ExpandByPoint](GUIBounds_ExpandByPoint.md) | `0x1403DF670` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Grows the double-precision draw bounds in the renderer (r+152..+176) to include one float point. |
| [GUICollisionNode_ContainsPoint](GUICollisionNode_ContainsPoint.md) | `0x1403FDCC8` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | The Collision node's shape test, in the node's local space. |
| [GUICollisionNode_EllipseContains](GUICollisionNode_EllipseContains.md) | `0x1403FDD54` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Ellipse / ring-sector point test: normalises the point into [-1,1] over the node's size, rejects outside the unit circle or inside innerRatio, and for sweep > 0 rejects the angular part measured from 'up'. |
| [GUIComponent_ApplyAnimChannels](GUIComponent_ApplyAnimChannels.md) | `0x1404015C0` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Component-level counterpart of GUINode_ApplyAnimChannels: samples the tracks of the channel set that is addressed to '#Component' (the component itself) and writes them into the component's effect fields. |
| [GUIComponent_ApplyStateAnims](GUIComponent_ApplyStateAnims.md) | `0x140409C28` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | For every active state of the component (vector comp+616 .. +624), evaluates the state's animation channels at the state's time and writes the sampled values onto the nodes (and the component itself) they target. |
| [GUIComponent_Ctor](GUIComponent_Ctor.md) | `0x1404087A4` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Base constructor for UISystem GUIComponent (vtable 0x1407225F8); layer node embedded at +272, kind at +536. |
| [GUIComponent_CursorLeave](GUIComponent_CursorLeave.md) | `0x14040A298` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Component virtual +48: the GUI cursor target moved away; plays CursorOut unless the component keeps its cursor look while focused, and posts event 13. |
| [GUIComponent_Disable](GUIComponent_Disable.md) | `0x140408FF8` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Mirror of GUIComponent_Enable: clears +554, plays the Disable state (Enable stopped) and posts event 11. |
| [GUIComponent_Enable](GUIComponent_Enable.md) | `0x140408EBC` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Marks a component enabled (+554 = 1) and plays its Enable state (Disable stopped); optionally enables the components its reference nodes point at. |
| [GUIComponent_GetFlag556](GUIComponent_GetFlag556.md) | `0x14040A52C` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Trivial virtual accessor: returns byte c+556. |
| [GUIComponent_Init](GUIComponent_Init.md) | `0x140408E78` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Initializes a component from its definition; copies the component object id (+8) into the layer node (+0x140). |
| [GUIComponent_LoadDef](GUIComponent_LoadDef.md) | `0x1403F5764` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Builds a runtime component from its UIB component record (0x40 bytes): property block, node tree, states. |
| [GUIComponent_LoadProps](GUIComponent_LoadProps.md) | `0x1403F4EC8` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Reads a component's property block from the UIB file into the runtime component: the enable byte and values of every effect group (colour transform, offsets, single float, drop shadow, glow, opacity, clip, mask). |
| [GUIComponent_OnClick](GUIComponent_OnClick.md) | `0x14040A444` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Component virtual +72: a completed click; plays the Click state and posts event 16. |
| [GUIComponent_OnDown](GUIComponent_OnDown.md) | `0x14040A324` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Component virtual +56: the confirm button / mouse button went down on the component; sets the pressed flag, plays the Down state and posts event 14. |
| [GUIComponent_OnFocusGained](GUIComponent_OnFocusGained.md) | `0x14040A534` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Component virtual +96: plays the component's FocusIn state (if the UIB has one; UnitJob has none, so nothing plays there) and posts event 19 to its owners. |
| [GUIComponent_OnFocusLost](GUIComponent_OnFocusLost.md) | `0x14040A5D4` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Component virtual +104: plays FocusOut and posts event 20 (JobTree_OnCursorLeave). |
| [GUIComponent_OnUp](GUIComponent_OnUp.md) | `0x14040A3B4` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Component virtual +64: the press ended; clears the pressed flag, plays the Up state and posts event 15. |
| [GUIComponent_ResetEffects](GUIComponent_ResetEffects.md) | `0x140408B40` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Resets a component to defaults: clears the effect enable bytes and frees its child list. |
| [GUIComponent_SetAllocatedSize](GUIComponent_SetAllocatedSize.md) | `0x1403F2EF8` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Stores the size taken from a reference node (ref+64 / +68) as the component's allocated size comp+528 / +532, swapped for the rotated layout mode, and resizes the component root when it asks for it. |
| [GUIComponent_StopAllStates](GUIComponent_StopAllStates.md) | `0x14040996C` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Stops every state of a component. |

| [GUIComponentCustom_Create](GUIComponentCustom_Create.md) | `0x140406048` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Allocates a 648-byte GUIComponentCustom from the UI pool (pages of 64), constructs it, links it into the owner's child list and registers it by id. |
| [GUIComponentCustom_CursorEnter](GUIComponentCustom_CursorEnter.md) | `0x14040A20C` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Cursor-enter handler of a custom component (vtable slot +40): plays the CursorOver state and tells the node's owners. |
| [GUIComponentGauge_Step](GUIComponentGauge_Step.md) | `0x14040DEC4` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | While the gauge value is animating (+688), eases three displayed fractions toward the target fraction using float tracks of the Increase / Decrease states, drives the GaugeBar / GaugeInc / GaugeDec states from them an... |
| [GUIComponentGauge_Update](GUIComponentGauge_Update.md) | `0x14040DE78` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Gauge component per-frame update (vtable slot 4): advances the states, runs the value animation step, applies tracks, updates the tree. |
| [GUIComponentList_FindSlotNode](GUIComponentList_FindSlotNode.md) | `0x14040C1DC` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Finds the layer node of slot number `slot`: the part named 'ListItem' for slot 0 and 'ListItem#n' for slot n. |
| [GUIComponentList_GetHScroll](GUIComponentList_GetHScroll.md) | `0x14040C0EC` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Same as GUIComponentList_GetVScroll for the horizontal bar (key +5064 = 'HScroll'). |
| [GUIComponentList_GetItemComponent](GUIComponentList_GetItemComponent.md) | `0x14040C270` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | The component currently showing item `item`, or null when that item is not in the shown window. |
| [GUIComponentList_GetSlotComponent](GUIComponentList_GetSlotComponent.md) | `0x14040C228` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | The component (a ListItem) behind a slot node: the slot node must be a reference node (kind 12). |
| [GUIComponentList_GetVScroll](GUIComponentList_GetVScroll.md) | `0x14040C080` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Returns the list's vertical scroll bar component (the 'VScroll' child reference node), or null. |
| [GUIComponentList_IsItemVisible](GUIComponentList_IsItemVisible.md) | `0x14040C2FC` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | True when the item's cell lies inside the list's viewport with at most half a cell cut off at each edge. |
| [GUIComponentList_ItemToSlot](GUIComponentList_ItemToSlot.md) | `0x14040C158` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Maps an item index to the recycled slot (a 'ListItem#n' node) that currently shows it, or -1 when the item is outside the window of shown items. |
| [GUIComponentList_LayoutSlots](GUIComponentList_LayoutSlots.md) | `0x14040CB60` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Places the recycled slot nodes of a list for the current scroll offset, re-binds slots that now show a different item (resetting their visuals and clearing focus / cursor on them) and posts event 31. |
| [GUIComponentList_SetItemCount](GUIComponentList_SetItemCount.md) | `0x14040CA1C` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Sets the number of list items; when it changed, invalidates the bound slots and marks both the scroll bars and the layout dirty. |
| [GUIComponentList_SetScrollX](GUIComponentList_SetScrollX.md) | `0x14040C8B0` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Sets the list's horizontal scroll offset, through the horizontal bar when there is one, and marks the layout dirty. |
| [GUIComponentList_SetScrollY](GUIComponentList_SetScrollY.md) | `0x14040C92C` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Vertical twin of GUIComponentList_SetScrollX: via the 'VScroll' bar, stores list+680 and sets the layout-dirty byte +685. |
| [GUIComponentList_UpdateScrollBars](GUIComponentList_UpdateScrollBars.md) | `0x14040CA50` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Gives the horizontal and vertical scroll bars their ranges from the item count and cell size, shows a bar only when the content is larger than the viewport, and clears the dirty byte +684. |
| [GUIComponentProps_Copy](GUIComponentProps_Copy.md) | `0x1403DF454` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Copies a component's 254-byte render-property block (the effect enable bytes and values: blend, colour transform, offsets, drop shadow, glow, clip and the two mask names) from one component to another. |
| [GUIComponentRef_Draw](GUIComponentRef_Draw.md) | `0x1403EDB20` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Draws a component reference node: finds the component, builds a draw context from its property block, opens an offscreen layer when the component has effects, draws its children, then composites the layer. |
| [GUIComponentRef_DrawChildren](GUIComponentRef_DrawChildren.md) | `0x1403E9A2C` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Draws the node tree of the component a reference node points at, or (when the layer mask path is active) just clears the current layer record's draw counter. |
| [GUIComponentScrollBar_StepKnob](GUIComponentScrollBar_StepKnob.md) | `0x14040BBF0` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | While the bar has a pending move (+681), eases the shown position toward the target using the ScrollUp / ScrollDown state tracks, then sizes and places the Knob part inside the Base part and posts event 27. |
| [GUIComponentScrollBar_Update](GUIComponentScrollBar_Update.md) | `0x14040BBA4` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Scroll bar per-frame update (vtable slot 4): advances the states, applies their tracks, repositions the knob and then updates the node tree. |
| [GUICore_BeginFrame](GUICore_BeginFrame.md) | `0x1403D6234` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Start of a UI frame in the GUI core: stores the frame times and the frame counters, advances the random state, refreshes the viewport transform and dispatches the queued events. |
| [GUICore_CommitFrame](GUICore_CommitFrame.md) | `0x1403D67D4` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Per-frame UI commit: resets the renderer, runs every widget's draw, then expands each layer's bounds for its effects. |
| [GUICore_Ctor](GUICore_Ctor.md) | `0x1403D5348` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Constructs the GUI core: widget lists, the UI renderer, the focus key strings, the screen size and the per-frame worker slots. |
| [GUICore_DispatchEvent](GUICore_DispatchEvent.md) | `0x1403D694C` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Hands one raw UI event to each registered handler (core+56..+64, vtable slot 1) until one consumes it, then to the core's own message handler (core+8). |
| [GUICore_DrawOutsideBars](GUICore_DrawOutsideBars.md) | `0x1403E5258` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Fills the part of the screen outside a transformed rectangle (the letterbox / pillarbox bars around the UI area) with a flat colour. |
| [GUICore_DrawWidget](GUICore_DrawWidget.md) | `0x1403E97A8` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Draws one widget: bumps the sort group when the widget's draw order word changes, fills the widget background, then builds the root draw context from the layer transform and draws the widget's main component tree. |
| [GUICore_FindWidgetByKey](GUICore_FindWidgetByKey.md) | `0x1403D69FC` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Finds the top-level widget that a key (name string + hash) names, walking the widget list; returns 0 when none matches. |
| [GUICore_FocusNode](GUICore_FocusNode.md) | `0x1401D2240` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Moves the GUI focus (and with it the finger cursor) onto a node, or clears the focus when the node is null. |
| [GUICore_GetFocus](GUICore_GetFocus.md) | `0x1403D6E28` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Resolves the focus key triple (core+1968) to the live widget and node; returns 0 when nothing is focused. |
| [GUICore_IsCursorTarget](GUICore_IsCursorTarget.md) | `0x1403D6BFC` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Same comparison as GUICore_IsFocusTarget against the cursor-target triple at core+1920 / +1936 / +1952. |
| [GUICore_IsFocusTarget](GUICore_IsFocusTarget.md) | `0x1403D6E8C` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Tests whether (widget, node) is the current GUI focus: compares the three keys (widget+16, node+504, node+280) with the focus triple at core+1968 / +1984 / +2000. |
| [GUICore_OnInputState](GUICore_OnInputState.md) | `0x1403D5A9C` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | The UI core's input listener: turns the frame's input state into queued UI events and then pumps the queue through GUICore_DispatchEvent. |
| [GUICore_SetCursorTarget](GUICore_SetCursorTarget.md) | `0x1403D6A64` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Makes a node the GUI cursor target: stores its key triple in the core's cursor slot and sends it the cursor-enter virtual call (the old target gets the cursor-leave call). |
| [GUICore_SetFocusComponent](GUICore_SetFocusComponent.md) | `0x1403D6CE0` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Moves focus to a component node: the old focus node gets virtual +104 (focus lost, event 20), the new one virtual +96 (focus gained, event 19); stores the new key triple at core+1968. |
| [GUICore_TickWidget](GUICore_TickWidget.md) | `0x1403D7670` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Gives one widget its frame time and calls its tick (vtable slot 7, GUIWidget_Tick). |
| [GUICore_UpdateFrame](GUICore_UpdateFrame.md) | `0x1403D7030` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Per-frame UI update (raw event 1): finds the node under the mouse pointer and moves hover/focus onto it. |
| [GUICore_UpdateWidgets](GUICore_UpdateWidgets.md) | `0x1403D76E4` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Per-frame widget update (the second GUICore update function; see GUICore_UpdateFrame for the other): orders the widgets, wires the ten frame worker slots when a task object exists and updates every widget. |
| [GUICursor_UpdateIndex](GUICursor_UpdateIndex.md) | `0x1400FE194` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Chooses the cursor index the UI wants (g_GUICore + 1904) and shows or hides the OS cursor. |
| [GUICursorHand_GetTargetPoint](GUICursorHand_GetTargetPoint.md) | `0x1401D2FF8` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | The hand's rest position for the focused component (left edge + hand+80, vertical centre + hand+84), or the screen centre (960, 540) when nothing is focused. |
| [GUICursorHand_StepToward](GUICursorHand_StepToward.md) | `0x1401D37D8` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | One step of the hand's travel: pos = roundf(pos + (target - pos) * k), per axis, written to the hand node. |
| [GUICursorHand_Update](GUICursorHand_Update.md) | `0x1401D2B00` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Per-frame update of the pointing-hand cursor: finds the focused component, computes where the hand belongs and moves it there. |
| [GuideBar_Layout](GuideBar_Layout.md) | `0x14013D758` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Rebuilds the guide bar: hides and resets all 8 slots, then lays out the visible rows of table 33 in two passes (left-aligned, then right-aligned). |
| [GuideBar_LayoutRow](GuideBar_LayoutRow.md) | `0x14013D844` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Configures one guide slot for table 33 row `row`: sets the text, the up to four button icons (keyboard / pad variants), places the slot at x and starts its on-animation. |
| [GuideBar_SetPage](GuideBar_SetPage.md) | `0x14013D5F4` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Selects the page of the button-guide bar: sets table key (+2224) and enabled-row bitmask (+2228) and rebuilds it with GuideBar_Layout. |

| [GUIFactory_FreeSlotGroup](GUIFactory_FreeSlotGroup.md) | `0x1403FECD0` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Returns a slot group to the pool: clears its occupancy bit, runs the group's destructor and, every time the live count crosses 32 modulo 64, drops empty pages. |

| [GUIFontSet_FontTypeForLanguage](GUIFontSet_FontTypeForLanguage.md) | `0x1403DCD50` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Picks the UIFontType id of a font-set row for the current game language. |

| [GuiImage_SetIconById](GuiImage_SetIconById.md) | `0x1402A8F40` | [Job data](../areas/job-data.md) | Binds an icon image, chosen by its Icon table key, to a UI image object; used by many screens that show job, command or ability icons. |
