| [sub_1400C3140](sub_1400C3140.md) | `0x1400C3140` | [Save data](../areas/save-data.md) | Turns one ready buffer into a named container entry: it seals the buffer with a CRC, queues it under the given name with version 17 and marks the object clean. |
| [sub_1400C3B84](sub_1400C3B84.md) | `0x1400C3B84` | [Save data](../areas/save-data.md) | The per-frame resume flush: it turns every dirty resume group into named entries, rotates the three-point ring and kicks the autosave container write. |
| [sub_1400C3DBC](sub_1400C3DBC.md) | `0x1400C3DBC` | [Save data](../areas/save-data.md) | Walks the resume name-to-record map and queues every dirty record as a container entry, erasing each record as it goes. |
| [sub_1400C3ED8](sub_1400C3ED8.md) | `0x1400C3ED8` | [Save data](../areas/save-data.md) | Packs one resume record under a given name: it ensures a map record exists for the name, copies the group's buffer into it, clears the group's ready byte and queues the entry. |
| [sub_1400C4198](sub_1400C4198.md) | `0x1400C4198` | [Save data](../areas/save-data.md) | Prepares and queues one named resume entry: formats the entry name from a part and a point, then hands the group to the packer. |
| [sub_1400C4214](sub_1400C4214.md) | `0x1400C4214` | [Save data](../areas/save-data.md) | Requests one resume point from the autosave container: the main part always, and optionally the world, attack and fturn parts, then kicks the read. |
| [sub_1400C42E0](sub_1400C42E0.md) | `0x1400C42E0` | [Save data](../areas/save-data.md) | Requests one named resume entry from the autosave container: it formats the name for a part and a point and queues the request on the group object. |
| [sub_1400C4360](sub_1400C4360.md) | `0x1400C4360` | [Save data](../areas/save-data.md) | Builds a resume entry name from a part index and a point index: `resume_enNN_<part>.sav`, `resume_enwm_main.sav` or `resume_enbtl_<part>.sav`. |
| [sub_1400C43D8](sub_1400C43D8.md) | `0x1400C43D8` | [Save data](../areas/save-data.md) | Returns the payload buffer and length of one part of the current resume point, so the Continue path can read the header without a copy. |
| [sub_1400C4690](sub_1400C4690.md) | `0x1400C4690` | [Save data](../areas/save-data.md) | The resume-order state machine: it walks the three-point ring at boot, requests each point's parts and keeps the newest three points in order. |
| [sub_1400C480C](sub_1400C480C.md) | `0x1400C480C` | [Save data](../areas/save-data.md) | The world-map save step: it flushes any ready resume record and, only when something was queued, kicks the autosave container write. |
| [sub_1400C6008](sub_1400C6008.md) | `0x1400C6008` | [Save data](../areas/save-data.md) | Fills a 50-slot save image with the empty-slot pattern, so a missing or rejected file reads as 50 empty slots instead of stale bytes. |
| [sub_1400D6FB0](sub_1400D6FB0.md) | `0x1400D6FB0` | [Items and shops](../areas/items.md) | Adjusts the count of one poach type, clamps it, and keeps the two ordered poach lists in step when a type enters or leaves the collection. |
| [sub_1400DC4C8](sub_1400DC4C8.md) | `0x1400DC4C8` | [Unit data](../areas/unit-data.md) | One of the battle targeting predicates: it decides whether a unit (or its target list) qualifies, and in this variant it accepts only a Mime and then walks the unit's stored target list through the caller's callback. |
| [sub_1400DDC70](sub_1400DDC70.md) | `0x1400DDC70` | [Unit data](../areas/unit-data.md) | The complement of the Mime predicate: it accepts a battle unit that is not a Mime and carries a particular marker, then walks the target list looking for an entry that is flagged. |
| [sub_1400E615C](sub_1400E615C.md) | `0x1400E615C` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | A guarded variant of the item-row text setter: it forwards an item id and four caller values to the item-row builder, but only when three UI subsystems are alive and the caller's kind is the one it serves. |
| [sub_1400ED328](sub_1400ED328.md) | `0x1400ED328` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Publishes a built list to the UI: it checks that the UI list module is open and hands the list parameters to the module's list setter with a "publish" flag. |
| [sub_1400ED3E8](sub_1400ED3E8.md) | `0x1400ED3E8` | [Items and shops](../areas/items.md) | Returns the equip screen's current slot selector: the byte at the UI object's `+0xF1C`, or 0 when the UI module is not up. |
| [sub_1400ED4BC](sub_1400ED4BC.md) | `0x1400ED4BC` | [Items and shops](../areas/items.md) | Returns the item screen's current list mode: the dword at the UI object's `+0x12EC`, or 0 when the module is not up. |
| [sub_1400EEC18](sub_1400EEC18.md) | `0x1400EEC18` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | The handler for a scene message word: it resolves the 5-bit class and 11-bit index of the word and dispatches to the text setter of that class. |
| [sub_1400F4BCC](sub_1400F4BCC.md) | `0x1400F4BCC` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Plays a UI sound cue: looks key soundKey up in UI table 0x45 and starts the sound the row names, dispatching on the row's type word. |
| [sub_1400F4C54](sub_1400F4C54.md) | `0x1400F4C54` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | The stop / state counterpart of sub_1400F4BCC: same table 0x45 lookup, but calls the stop-type function of each of the four handlers. |
| [sub_1400F4D38](sub_1400F4D38.md) | `0x1400F4D38` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Event handler (slot 2) of the UIDerivedWidget vtable at 0x140704578: forwards the event to the base handler, then plays the widget's own press / release cue when the event is type 12 or 16 and the owner is in the righ... |
| [sub_1400F5674](sub_1400F5674.md) | `0x1400F5674` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Focuses a GUI node (GUICore_FocusNode) with its byte +557 temporarily cleared while any 'character render' slot is active, so the focus switch does not trigger the node's 557 behaviour. |
| [sub_1400F5B0C](sub_1400F5B0C.md) | `0x1400F5B0C` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Moves the cursor on a list of GUI nodes: optionally focuses nodes[cursor], then updates every node's CursorOver / CursorOut animation (sub_1400F5C78) with 'is cursor' = (index == cursor). |
| [sub_1400F5BCC](sub_1400F5BCC.md) | `0x1400F5BCC` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Same as sub_1400F5B0C but takes the option struct as a pointer instead of two flags. |
| [sub_1400F6148](sub_1400F6148.md) | `0x1400F6148` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Three-way (-1 / 0 / 1) animation switch for a node with two named states (default A = qword_143CDA2A0+5240 / +4600 pair, B = +5256 / +4616 pair): plays one, stops the other, and sets node+416 accordingly. |
| [sub_1400F69BC](sub_1400F69BC.md) | `0x1400F69BC` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Looks up the button icon / name string for a control action id: the icon a key / pad rebinding table gives for (actionId, slot), else the default one from UI table 25. |
| [sub_1400F6B5C](sub_1400F6B5C.md) | `0x1400F6B5C` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Resolves a button id through an Nxd table (qword_143CDA2C0, found by NxdRegistry_Find index 35) and returns one of four glyph ids according to the player's pad layout (byte at qword_143CD9EE8 + 13726). |
| [sub_1400F6D58](sub_1400F6D58.md) | `0x1400F6D58` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Steps a cursor value by one with wrap-around when the 'vertical' direction pair is pressed (actions 35 / 36, masks 0x8000 / 0x2000), via sub_1400F6FA0. |
| [sub_1400F6DD4](sub_1400F6DD4.md) | `0x1400F6DD4` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Same as sub_1400F6D58 but for the other direction pair (actions 33 / 34, masks 0x1000 / 0x4000) via sub_1400F704C. |
| [sub_1400F6FA0](sub_1400F6FA0.md) | `0x1400F6FA0` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Reads the second direction pair (actions 35 = -1 and 36 = +1, masks 0x8000 and 0x2000): reports a step and whether it may wrap. |
| [sub_1400F704C](sub_1400F704C.md) | `0x1400F704C` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | First direction pair: actions 33 = -1 (mask 0x1000 = 4096) and 34 = +1 (mask 0x4000), same protocol as sub_1400F6FA0. |
| [sub_1400F7378](sub_1400F7378.md) | `0x1400F7378` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Fills one button-guide entry of a panel: from row (tableKey, rowKey) of UI table 33 sets the IconButton part's label key and the Title text id. |
| [sub_1400FA7AC](sub_1400FA7AC.md) | `0x1400FA7AC` | [Unit data](../areas/unit-data.md) | Adds an equipped item's attribute record and added-effect statuses to a unit's status state. |
| [sub_1400FA94C](sub_1400FA94C.md) | `0x1400FA94C` | [Battle formulas](../areas/formulas.md) | Fills an effect description (out) from a usable item's or an action's added-effect record. |
| [sub_1400FABC4](sub_1400FABC4.md) | `0x1400FABC4` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Two-pane grid cursor navigation (5 columns per row): moves a (pane, index) cursor with up / down / left / right and pane switch, and returns the flat index across both panes. |
| [sub_1400FB93C](sub_1400FB93C.md) | `0x1400FB93C` | [Items and shops](../areas/items.md) | Fills a caller structure with an item's record and its category sub-record: the numbers the equip detail panel prints. |
| [sub_1400FB9F0](sub_1400FB9F0.md) | `0x1400FB9F0` | [Items and shops](../areas/items.md) | Classifies an item into the equipment slot group it belongs to, and writes the id and the group into a two-dword structure. |
| [sub_1400FC29C](sub_1400FC29C.md) | `0x1400FC29C` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Detects an 'opposite directions held at once' state on the pad / keyboard direction set: returns true when two opposite inputs of any axis are down together, which makes direction stepping ignore input. |
| [sub_1400FC6D8](sub_1400FC6D8.md) | `0x1400FC6D8` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | 'Was this action pressed this frame' test for the direction helpers: checks the action's pressed / repeat flags, with an optional key-mask test. |
| [sub_1400FC85C](sub_1400FC85C.md) | `0x1400FC85C` | [Data access](../areas/data.md) | Identifies which text pack and which text kind a string-pool pointer belongs to. |
| [sub_1400FCE48](sub_1400FCE48.md) | `0x1400FCE48` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Checks that no 'busy' gate is active: returns 1 when the global gate variables at 0x142FF9F24..0x142FF9F50 are all clear (or the alternative gate function says OK). |
| [sub_1400FE810](sub_1400FE810.md) | `0x1400FE810` | [Resource](../areas/resource.md) | Appends one 16-byte element to a segmented container whose control block sits at a fixed global plus 0xF8. |
| [sub_14010824C](sub_14010824C.md) | `0x14010824C` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | The Land ability help panel's content method: it reads the Land row's ability id and icon and fills the panel's text and icon slots. |
| [sub_140109134](sub_140109134.md) | `0x140109134` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Refreshes a battle-side panel object from a packed text id or from the panel's own selection fields. |
| [sub_140109F0C](sub_140109F0C.md) | `0x140109F0C` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | A smaller sibling of sub_140109134: it finds one named text part of a UI object and fills it from a packed text id, choosing item, ability or a third source by the text kind. |
| [sub_140130DF0](sub_140130DF0.md) | `0x140130DF0` | [Job tree](../areas/job-tree.md) | Input handler of the job-list panel for mode 2 (the main cell grid): confirm / cancel / detail buttons and cursor movement across the 19 cells. |
| [sub_140131068](sub_140131068.md) | `0x140131068` | [Job tree](../areas/job-tree.md) | Input handler of the job-list panel for mode 3 (the job action / confirmation list at p+5296..p+5320): confirm / cancel / list movement. |
| [sub_140131688](sub_140131688.md) | `0x140131688` | [Job tree](../areas/job-tree.md) | Handles the info / help button (action 0x7E = 126) in the job-list panel: shows a message popup for the cursor cell or emits an addon event. |
| [sub_140131734](sub_140131734.md) | `0x140131734` | [Job tree](../areas/job-tree.md) | Handles action 0x8E (142) on a cell that has the flag byte at [148 * cell + p + 8974]: shows the info popup 37 when the cell state is 2, else sets out[0] = 1410 and plays the refusal sound. |
| [sub_1401319FC](sub_1401319FC.md) | `0x1401319FC` | [Job tree](../areas/job-tree.md) | Plays the Show / Hide animation of the sub-widget at p+4912 (the tab strip at p+4896) and, when showing, initialises its cursor. |
| [sub_1401321FC](sub_1401321FC.md) | `0x1401321FC` | [Job list and unlock](../areas/job-list.md) | Refreshes the job-tree panel's job rows: it walks the GeneralJob prerequisite lists, maps each job id to its index and writes the panel's name, icon and prerequisite text. |
| [sub_140132B34](sub_140132B34.md) | `0x140132B34` | [Job tree](../areas/job-tree.md) | Copies the current cell's data into a 124-byte preview record and calls sub_1402A7474 (update the detail view for that cell). |
| [sub_140132CF4](sub_140132CF4.md) | `0x140132CF4` | [Job tree](../areas/job-tree.md) | Posts command 123 to the addon's owner with argument 1024 or 1025 depending on sub_1400FCE48 (busy gate). |
| [sub_140132D6C](sub_140132D6C.md) | `0x140132D6C` | [Job tree](../areas/job-tree.md) | Refreshes the sprite palette state of all 19 cells after the cursor / mode changed. |
| [sub_140132DC8](sub_140132DC8.md) | `0x140132DC8` | [Job tree](../areas/job-tree.md) | Clears the 'tinted' flag of a cell's chara sprite: restores palette bits and re-requests the render slot. |
| [sub_140132E1C](sub_140132E1C.md) | `0x140132E1C` | [Job tree](../areas/job-tree.md) | Applies the 'unavailable / dimmed' palette to a cell's chara sprite (palette bit group 6) when the cell is the cursor cell with state 0 or sub_140132D48 says so, and marks the cell tinted. |
| [sub_1401332A0](sub_1401332A0.md) | `0x1401332A0` | [Job tree](../areas/job-tree.md) | Sets the visibility byte (+144) of the first kind-13 part of every one of the 19 cell nodes and of one extra node. |
| [sub_140133DB8](sub_140133DB8.md) | `0x140133DB8` | [Job tree](../areas/job-tree.md) | UnitJobTree addon vtable slot 7: posts command 126 to the addon owner. |
| [sub_140134B54](sub_140134B54.md) | `0x140134B54` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Focus-change handler (slot 27) of the UnitStatusHoroscopeAffinity addon: activates / deactivates the addon when focus enters or leaves id 1111 / 1517. |
| [sub_140134DA8](sub_140134DA8.md) | `0x140134DA8` | [Items and shops](../areas/items.md) | Resolves one row of a small UI table from the key held on a screen object and a mode index, and returns the row's dword at `+0x0C`. |
| [sub_1401365AC](sub_1401365AC.md) | `0x1401365AC` | [Event system](../areas/event-system.md) | Gives the input focus to one of the two option nodes of a choice window: the node the message window task calls "the cursor line" while a story choice waits. |
| [sub_140136654](sub_140136654.md) | `0x140136654` | [Event system](../areas/event-system.md) | Shows the finger cursor over the focused option of a choice window once the window's nodes are all built. |
| [sub_14013DD70](sub_14013DD70.md) | `0x14013DD70` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Fades a guide slot in or out: finds the slot of row `row`, sets its node visible byte and plays Gauge_on / Gauge_off. |
| [sub_140142014](sub_140142014.md) | `0x140142014` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Builds one row of an item list: its name text, the item's small picture path and the parts that draw them. |
| [sub_14014D57C](sub_14014D57C.md) | `0x14014D57C` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Fills a selection panel from one item: clears its text, stores the id, updates a named node, and sets the small item picture and name on the panel's parts. |
| [sub_140153CA0](sub_140153CA0.md) | `0x140153CA0` | [Items and shops](../areas/items.md) | Binds one item or ability to a UI cell: it sets the cell's icon path and name from a 16-bit text id, reads the inventory count for an item, and records the cell state. |
| [sub_1401560D0](sub_1401560D0.md) | `0x1401560D0` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Fills the four command radio buttons of a command screen: each button gets the icon and the title of the skillset named by its text token (name token 0xB000 \| set), so it is the one place a skillset's name is read str... |
| [sub_14016E60C](sub_14016E60C.md) | `0x14016E60C` | [Errands](../areas/errands.md) | Event handler of an errand screen: on event 0x10001 shows the two treasure-progress gauges (milestone counts). |
| [sub_140183658](sub_140183658.md) | `0x140183658` | [Save data](../areas/save-data.md) | The world-map screen's update callback, in two modes: build and show the map panel, or trigger the world-map save. |
| [sub_14018373C](sub_14018373C.md) | `0x14018373C` | [Save data](../areas/save-data.md) | The load screen's resume-header table: it builds the small object that holds one summary per resume point and fills one entry from each delivered resume payload. |
| [sub_1401839E4](sub_1401839E4.md) | `0x1401839E4` | [Save data](../areas/save-data.md) | The load-confirmation handler: it takes the selected slot, asks for its resume parts, loads the slot into the live state and, when the slot was saved in a battle, starts the resume path instead of a plain load. |
| [sub_140183C38](sub_140183C38.md) | `0x140183C38` | [Save data](../areas/save-data.md) | The save-confirmation handler: it writes the chosen slot from live state, then updates the screen and plays the confirmation cue. |
| [sub_140183DD4](sub_140183DD4.md) | `0x140183DD4` | [Save data](../areas/save-data.md) | Deletes one manual save slot: it fills the slot's 40,164-byte work copy with the empty pattern, copies it into the cache and marks the cache dirty. |
| [sub_14018403C](sub_14018403C.md) | `0x14018403C` | [Save data](../areas/save-data.md) | The load-screen setup callback: in mode 0 it reads the whole manual-save container, and in mode 1 it advances the screen when there is something to show. |
| [sub_140197BCC](sub_140197BCC.md) | `0x140197BCC` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | The new-item flag handler for the item screens: it clears or focuses the per-item new-arrival flag that marks an item the player has not looked at yet. |
| [sub_1401A161C](sub_1401A161C.md) | `0x1401A161C` | [Errands](../areas/errands.md) | The menu screen's decide step: it counts the entries in a list owned by the screen, reads the selected record's kind and switches the screen to the matching sub-state or help page. |
| [sub_1401ABE14](sub_1401ABE14.md) | `0x1401ABE14` | [Items and shops](../areas/items.md) | Rebuilds the row list of the Poachers' Den screen from the saved poach ordering, taking a vector of 40-byte entry records as its input. |
| [sub_1401AEEF0](sub_1401AEEF0.md) | `0x1401AEEF0` | [Items and shops](../areas/items.md) | Refreshes the equip and possession detail panel for one item, including its shop-new flag, and rebuilds the panel's text and icon slots. |
| [sub_1401D1E30](sub_1401D1E30.md) | `0x1401D1E30` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Activates the addon's root widget (a+24) through sub_1403F930C and marks the GUI core dirty. |
| [sub_1401D1E70](sub_1401D1E70.md) | `0x1401D1E70` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Returns whether the addon's first kind-1 component has the active-state flag set (state +4600 playing / done byte +105). |
| [sub_1401D1EAC](sub_1401D1EAC.md) | `0x1401D1EAC` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Deactivates the addon's root widget (a+24) through sub_1403F93F8. |
| [sub_1401D2344](sub_1401D2344.md) | `0x1401D2344` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Default addon handler (used by about 130 addon vtables): walks the root widget's child list and advances each child's three-stage readiness byte (+573), registering the addon id in the child's owner list. |
| [sub_1401D2E9C](sub_1401D2E9C.md) | `0x1401D2E9C` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Slot 6 of the UIAgentCursor vtable: reacts to an event addressed to the cursor's target node. |
| [sub_1401D80A8](sub_1401D80A8.md) | `0x1401D80A8` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Sets one UI slot's cue id and dispatches it: the slot number selects the subsystem (sound, text dispatch, and others) and the id selects what that subsystem plays or shows. |
| [sub_1401D9174](sub_1401D9174.md) | `0x1401D9174` | [Event system](../areas/event-system.md) | Starts a background music transition: looks the transition up in the BGMTransition table and binds the music id of the sound entry it names to slot 1. |
| [sub_1401DEC14](sub_1401DEC14.md) | `0x1401DEC14` | [Event system](../areas/event-system.md) | Walks the inline text of an event instruction: it steps byte by byte, handles the three control codes it knows, measures each text run and advances to the next instruction. |
| [sub_1401DF120](sub_1401DF120.md) | `0x1401DF120` | [Event system](../areas/event-system.md) | Answers whether a text needs two or more lines at a given width: it decodes the text, measures it and divides the measurement by the width. |
| [sub_1401E1618](sub_1401E1618.md) | `0x1401E1618` | [Event system](../areas/event-system.md) | One step of the event command task: it starts the task, runs one queued command per step, and tears the task down when the script ends. |
| [sub_1401E7EF8](sub_1401E7EF8.md) | `0x1401E7EF8` | [Save data](../areas/save-data.md) | Probes the SYSTEM save area for `settings.sav` while the title and continue paths set themselves up, and applies what it reads. |
| [sub_1401EA910](sub_1401EA910.md) | `0x1401EA910` | [Unit data](../areas/unit-data.md) | Adds an OverrideEntryData row's HP and MP growth bonuses to a unit work record, by an index from a global record. |
| [sub_1401EC01C](sub_1401EC01C.md) | `0x1401EC01C` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Walks the forty battle message entries and resolves each one through the shared resolver. |
| [sub_1401F3858](sub_1401F3858.md) | `0x1401F3858` | [UI framework](../areas/ui-framework.md) | Refreshes a party/unit panel: it looks up the unit's sprite shape and shows or hides the lock and starting-team icons. |
| [sub_1401F92A8](sub_1401F92A8.md) | `0x1401F92A8` | [Battle](../areas/battle.md) | Fills the 106 rows of the PostBattleCalc object, one per formula id: the 12 formulas with an after-damage rule get a hook row, every other row holds the do-nothing marker. |
